<div>
<h2>Change your perspective in Bhutan</h2>
<p>Newly emerging onto the world stage, the Land of the Thunder Dragon is calling. The Six-Night Khamsa (royal stroll) around three Six Senses Bhutan lodges balances meaningful encounters and experiences with a healthy dose of fun.</p>
<p>
<a class="button-link" href="/en/corporate/news/change-your-perspective-in-bhutan">Read more</a>
</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</li>
<li data-thumb-alt="" style="width: 705px; margin-right: 0px; float: left; display: block;">
<div>
<div class="item">
<div>
<picture>
<source media="(max-width: 768px)" srcset="/media/7442/six_senses_loire_valley-hires.jpg?anchor=center&amp;mode=crop&amp;width=648&amp;height=648&amp;rnd=132077412380000000"/>
<source media="(min-width: 769px) and (max-width: 1024px)" srcset="/media/7442/six_senses_loire_valley-hires.jpg?anchor=center&amp;mode=crop&amp;width=510&amp;height=510&amp;rnd=132077412380000000"/>
<img alt="Life’s not always a beach" draggable="false" src="/media/7442/six_senses_loire_valley-hires.jpg?anchor=center&amp;mode=crop&amp;width=790&amp;height=790&amp;rnd=132077412380000000"/>
</picture>
</div>
<div>
<h2>Life’s not always a beach</h2>
<p>Six Senses has a track record for discovering out-of-the ordinary destinations. If your habitual vacation ventures no further than a beach towel, why not dust off the sand and set off for forts, skyscrapers, chateaux and mystical elves!</p>
<p>
<a class="button-link" href="/en/corporate/news/life-s-not-always-a-beach">Read more</a>
</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</li>
<li data-thumb-alt="" style="width: 705px; margin-right: 0px; float: left; display: block;">
<div>
<div class="item">
<div>
<picture>
<source media="(max-width: 768px)" srcset="/media/7959/news-2-main.jpg?anchor=center&amp;mode=crop&amp;width=648&amp;height=648&amp;rnd=132122465180000000"/>
<source media="(min-width: 769px) and (max-width: 1024px)" srcset="/media/7959/news-2-main.jpg?anchor=center&amp;mode=crop&amp;width=510&amp;height=510&amp;rnd=132122465180000000"/>
<img alt="Think outside the (bird) box in Cambodia" draggable="false" src="/media/7959/news-2-main.jpg?anchor=center&amp;mode=crop&amp;width=790&amp;height=790&amp;rnd=132122465180000000"/>
</picture>
</div>
<div>
<h2>Think outside the (bird) box in Cambodia</h2>
<p>Few things pull you out of a funk like cheerful birdsong or the appearance of a beautiful butterfly. Knowing that nature is essential to human health, Six Senses Krabey Island has recently launched a bird and butterfly watching experience.</p>
<p>
<a class="button-link" href="/en/corporate/news/think-outside-the-bird-box-in-cambodia">Read more</a>
</p>

<h2>The magic behind the brand</h2>
<p>To mark Travel + Leisure readers voting Six Senses as World’s No. 1 Hotel Brand for the third year running, CEO Neil Jacobs thanks our hosts for creating the magic behind each meaningful guest experience. We hope you agree!</p>

<p class="category"><a data-udi="umb://document/2c3d04fdb87247b08dce9c490b28ae9c" href="/en/sustainability" title="Sustainability">Six Senses Sustainability</a></p>
<h2><a data-udi="umb://document/2c3d04fdb87247b08dce9c490b28ae9c" href="/en/sustainability" title="Sustainability">Measurable actions for positive impact</a></h2>
<p>We care deeply about our people and the cultures and ecosystems we are part of, and want you to experience the magic first hand. Everything from helping us grow our own organic produce to learning how we close the loop on waste through to replanting coral and reforestation projects.</p>
<ul class="buttons" data-macro="BoxLinks" data-timestamp="637074210166861342">

<h2><a data-udi="umb://document/b84e3e3bfb1949fe8a645a6546180469" href="/en/wellness-spa" title="Wellness &amp; Spa">Say hello to a whole new you</a></h2>

<p class="category">
About Six Senses
</p>
<h2>
What’s the story of Six Senses?
</h2>
<p>It’s about natural beauty that stops you in your tracks. Far flung, yet accessible locations. A sense of community with sustainability at its heart. A level of service that doesn’t just do the right thing, but that does it well, and with wellness in mind, so you feel the difference in your mind, body and soul.</p>
<p class="link-button">
<a href="/en/about-us" target="_self">Read more</a>
</p>

<div>
<h2><a href="https://www.instagram.com/sixsenseshotelsresortsspas" target="_blank">#OutOfTheOrdinary</a></h2>
<p>Out of the ordinary describes something that you would not ordinarily do, but had a blast doing at Six Senses. It’s about discovery of your fun side, your emotional side and your connection and reconnection with yourself and those around you.<br/><br/>Showcase your special images by including the hashtag <a href="https://www.instagram.com/sixsenseshotelsresortsspas" target="_blank">#OutOfTheOrdinary</a> with any posts on social media and connect with the world.</p>
